# distributed cleanup report

Phase 9; base `d06d00ff68bdedd5c764ec0bdbff7526c315d2f0`.

Retired unwired peer observation/registry prototypes and consolidated live admission contracts without removing the production trust chain.

| Original file | Decision | Responsibility and usage evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `__init__.py` | KEEP | Distributed-node evidence contracts; no network operations or authority. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `agent.py` | KEEP | Pinned mutual-TLS remote fleet agent with typed operations only. Imported by `distributed/agent_main.py`, `distributed/agent_tools.py`, `distributed/windows_agent_service.py`. |
| `agent_main.py` | KEEP | Explicit foreground entry point for Sofía's pinned mTLS fleet agent. Imported by `distributed/windows_agent_service.py`, `distributed/windows_agent_service_admin.py`. |
| `agent_tools.py` | KEEP | Default typed operations exposed by the fleet agent. Imported by `distributed/agent_main.py`. |
| `authorization.py` | KEEP | Immutable RemoteGrant and abstract RemoteAuthorization are consumed by live gateway/durable admission. Retired the test-only in-memory grant implementation; security tests now use DurableRemoteAuthorization. |
| `capabilities.py` | KEEP | Engineering 22E: remote capability claims and evidence freshness. Imported by `distributed/agent.py`, `distributed/authorization.py`, `distributed/durable.py`, `distributed/https_transport.py`, `distributed/inference_control.py`, `distributed/operations.py`. |
| `capability.py` | KEEP | Cognitive tools for authenticated, exact-grant remote fleet operations. Imported by `composition/root.py`. |
| `durable.py` | KEEP | Production exact-scope grants and replay ledger; removed allocation of unused in-memory grant dictionary in durable subclass. |
| `endpoint_bound_gateway.py` | KEEP | Owns both live identity and exact endpoint admission wrappers before durable authorization/replay checks. |
| `endpoint_policy.py` | MERGE | ApprovedEndpoint moved unchanged to model.py; obsolete in-memory EndpointPolicy retired in favor of durable endpoint approvals. |
| `endpoint_policy_durable.py` | KEEP | Durable exact endpoint approvals for PKG-NET. | `inference_client.py` | KEEP | Configured production client for authenticated Fleet cognitive inference. Imported by `composition/root.py`. |

| `operator.py` | KEEP | Host-side operator commands for secure fleet enrollment, endpoints and grants. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `pki.py` | KEEP | Operator-only PKI provisioning for pinned mutual-TLS Fleet links. Imported by `ops/windows_rekey_bootstrap.py`. |
| `reachability.py` | DELETE | Offline reachability prototype was referenced only by its own tests; live discovery uses authenticated Fleet probes. |
| `remote_control.py` | KEEP | Production admission composition for distributed operations. Imported by `distributed/capability.py`, `distributed/fleet_probe.py`, `distributed/inference_client.py`. |
| `retirement.py` | DELETE | Unwired NodeRetirementCoordinator duplicated operator.retire_node. Retirement regression now invokes the real operator against canonical SQLite. |

| `systemd_agent_service.py` | KEEP | systemd service packaging for Sofía's Fleet agent on Linux. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `tls.py` | KEEP | Pinned public-key helpers for authenticated fleet TLS. Imported by `distributed/agent.py`, `distributed/https_transport.py`, `distributed/operator.py`, `distributed/pki.py`, `ops/agent_discovery.py`, `ops/windows_rekey_bootstrap.py`. |
| `version.py` | KEEP | Defines FleetProtocolVersion. Imported by `application/release_runtime.py`, `config/model.py`, `dev/release_cli.py`, `distributed/agent.py`, `distributed/capabilities.py`, `distributed/https_transport.py`, `ops/agent_discovery.py`, `ops/bootstrap.py`, `run/release.py`. |
| `windows_agent_service.py` | KEEP | Windows SCM host for Sofía's pinned mTLS Fleet agent. Imported by `distributed/windows_agent_service_admin.py`. |
| `windows_agent_service_admin.py` | KEEP | Install/update/remove/validate the Windows Fleet-agent service. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |

Production paths: composition.root → distributed.capability → configured mTLS transport / identity and endpoint policies; application.background_runtime → OPS discovery → fleet_probe; cognition Fleet engine → inference_client → durable inference admission; agent_main/windows_agent_service → authenticated agent server and configured agent tools. String-invoked module CLIs (operator, agent_main, Windows service administration) remain explicit operator entry points. Enrollment and approval validation bodies and IdentityBoundGateway were compared by AST before/after relocation. No deleted compatibility shims remain. Node retire regression checks durable identity and endpoint denial; existing grant revocation tests cover grants/restart. Removed tests exclusively asserting retired prototypes; retained enrollment and endpoint validation tests.

## Validation and remaining scope

Compile passed. Distributed/remote mTLS/OPS/application Fleet/cognitive routing/tool completion gate: 338 passed. Full suite: 3331 passed, 6 failed, 2 skipped (66.66s). Five failures require unavailable local Ollama; the sixth is the known Windows layout fixture, scheduled for config in Phase 10. Source/test/tools search found no retired names or import paths. No live remote host or Windows service was available; acceptance tests use controlled mTLS agents and platform fixtures.

DurableNodeIdentityRegistry, DurableEndpointPolicy and DurableRemoteAuthorization own production identity/approvals/grants in canonical_fleet_state_paths; DurableRemoteLedger and DurableInferenceLedger own their respective replay records. Agent-local persistence remains host-local. RemoteAuthorization is an abstract interface and owns no grant state; DurableRemoteAuthorization is the only implementation in production source.

Checkpoint: `git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/development/distributed-cleanup-report.md`.
